; Slow 1100mV 85C Model Fmax Summary                                                                 ;
+------------+-----------------+--------------------------------------------------------------+------+
; Fmax       ; Restricted Fmax ; Clock Name                                                   ; Note ;
+------------+-----------------+--------------------------------------------------------------+------+
; 65.38 MHz  ; 65.38 MHz       ; u_pll|altera_pll_i|general[3].gpll~PLL_OUTPUT_COUNTER|divclk ;      ;
; 68.2 MHz   ; 68.2 MHz        ; altera_reserved_tck                                          ;      ;
; 184.57 MHz ; 184.57 MHz      ; u_pll|altera_pll_i|general[0].gpll~PLL_OUTPUT_COUNTER|divclk ;      ;
+------------+-----------------+--------------------------------------------------------------+------+
This panel reports FMAX for every clock in the design, regardless of the user-specified clock periods.  FMAX is only computed for paths where the source and destination registers or ports are driven by the same clock.  Paths of different clocks, including generated clocks, are ignored.  For paths between a clock and its inversion, FMAX is computed as if the rising and falling edges are scaled along with FMAX, such that the duty cycle (in terms of a percentage) is maintained. Altera recommends that you always use clock constraints and other slack reports for sign-off analysis.

Shortest Synchronizer Chain: 1 Registers
Fraction of Chains for which MTBFs Could Not be Calculated: 0.897
Worst Case Available Settling Time: 8.142 ns

Worst-Case MTBF values are calculated based on the worst-case silicon characteristics, with worst-case operating conditions.
  - Under worst-case conditions, an increase of 100ps in available settling time will increase MTBF values by a factor of 7.8
Typical MTBF values are calculated based on the nominal silicon characteristics, at nominal operating conditions.
  - Under typical conditions, an increase of 100ps in available settling time will increase MTBF values by a factor of 17.8
